cat > /workspace/scripts/train.py <<'EOF'
# LoRA fine-tuning on the built dataset, merged back into the base weights.
# PYTHONPATH=/workspace/pylib /opt/venv/bin/python3 train.py --data D.jsonl --out DIR
import argparse, json, math, os, random, sys, time
import torch
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model

ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
ap.add_argument("--data", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--out", required=True)
ap.add_argument("--epochs", type=float, default=3)
ap.add_argument("--lr", type=float, default=2e-4)
ap.add_argument("--r", type=int, default=64)
ap.add_argument("--alpha", type=int, default=128)
ap.add_argument("--tok_per_step", type=int, default=32768)
ap.add_argument("--max_len", type=int, default=4096)
ap.add_argument("--targets", default="q_proj,k_proj,v_proj,o_proj,in_proj_qkv,in_proj_z,out_proj,gate_proj,up_proj,down_proj")
ap.add_argument("--save_every_epoch", action="store_true")
a = ap.parse_args()
os.makedirs(a.out, exist_ok=True)
M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(M)
torch.manual_seed(0); random.seed(0)

rows = [json.loads(l) for l in open(a.data)]
def encode(r):
    if r["kind"] == "text":
        ids = tok(r["text"], add_special_tokens=False).input_ids + [tok.convert_tokens_to_ids("<|endoftext|>")]
        return ids, [1] * len(ids)
    msgs = [{"role": "user", "content": r["q"]}]
    p = tok.apply_chat_template(msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=r.get("think", False))
    pi = tok(p, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    ri = tok(r["a"] + "<|im_end|>\n", add_special_tokens=False).input_ids
    return pi + ri, [0] * len(pi) + [1] * len(ri)
ex = []
for r in rows:
    ids, m = encode(r)
    if len(ids) > a.max_len:
        continue
    ex.append((ids, m, r.get("w", 1.0)))
print("examples", len(ex), "tokens", sum(len(e[0]) for e in ex), flush=True)

def batches():
    order = list(range(len(ex))); random.shuffle(order)
    # bucket by length in chunks to keep padding low but batches mixed
    out = []
    for c in range(0, len(order), 4096):
        chunk = sorted(order[c:c + 4096], key=lambda i: len(ex[i][0]))
        cur, mx = [], 0
        for i in chunk:
            L = len(ex[i][0])
            if cur and max(mx, L) * (len(cur) + 1) > a.tok_per_step:
                out.append(cur); cur, mx = [], 0
            cur.append(i); mx = max(mx, L)
        if cur: out.append(cur)
    random.shuffle(out)
    return out

model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(M, dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda()
model.config.use_cache = False
model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs={"use_reentrant": False})
model.enable_input_require_grads()
cfg = LoraConfig(r=a.r, lora_alpha=a.alpha, lora_dropout=0.0, target_modules=a.targets.split(","), bias="none")
model = get_peft_model(model, cfg)
for n, p in model.named_parameters():
    if p.requires_grad:
        p.data = p.data.float()
model.print_trainable_parameters()
params = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
opt = torch.optim.AdamW(params, lr=a.lr, weight_decay=0.0, betas=(0.9, 0.99))
nb = len(batches())
total = int(nb * a.epochs)
warm = max(10, total // 30)
sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.LambdaLR(opt, lambda s: min(1, (s + 1) / warm) * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * min(1, s / total))))
print("steps/epoch", nb, "total", total, flush=True)
pad = tok.pad_token_id
step, t0 = 0, time.time()
model.train()
ep = 0
while step < total:
    for b in batches():
        if step >= total: break
        L = max(len(ex[i][0]) for i in b)
        ids = torch.full((len(b), L), pad, dtype=torch.long)
        lab = torch.full((len(b), L), -100, dtype=torch.long)
        att = torch.zeros((len(b), L), dtype=torch.long)
        w = torch.zeros((len(b), L))
        for j, i in enumerate(b):
            x, m, wt = ex[i]
            ids[j, :len(x)] = torch.tensor(x); att[j, :len(x)] = 1
            mm = torch.tensor(m)
            lab[j, :len(x)] = torch.where(mm.bool(), torch.tensor(x), torch.tensor(-100))
            w[j, :len(x)] = mm.float() * wt
        ids, lab, att, w = ids.cuda(), lab.cuda(), att.cuda(), w.cuda()
        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
            logits = model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).logits
        lg = logits[:, :-1].float(); tg = lab[:, 1:]; ww = w[:, 1:]
        nll = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(lg.reshape(-1, lg.size(-1)), tg.reshape(-1), ignore_index=-100, reduction="none").view_as(tg)
        loss = (nll * ww).sum() / ww.sum().clamp(min=1)
        loss.backward()
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)
        opt.step(); sched.step(); opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        step += 1
        if step % 10 == 0:
            print(f"step {step}/{total} loss {loss.item():.4f} lr {sched.get_last_lr()[0]:.2e} {time.time()-t0:.0f}s", flush=True)
    ep += 1
    if a.save_every_epoch and step < total:
        model.save_pretrained(f"{a.out}/lora_ep{ep}")
model.save_pretrained(f"{a.out}/lora_final")
print("TRAINED", time.time() - t0, flush=True)
# merge in fp32 and export
model = model.merge_and_unload()
sys.path.insert(0, "/task/eval")
from export import export_submission
export_submission(model, f"{a.out}/model.safetensors")
print("EXPORTED", flush=True)

# Local probe eval with the verifier's generation code. /opt/venv/bin/python3 eval_local.py MODEL.safetensors EVAL.jsonl [OUT.jsonl]
import json, sys, collections, time
sys.path.insert(0, "/task")
import contract
from pathlib import Path
mf, ef = Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
items = [json.loads(l) for l in open(ef)]
t0 = time.time()
model, tok = contract.load_model(mf)
res = collections.defaultdict(lambda: [0, 0])
outs = []
groups = collections.defaultdict(list)
for it in items:
    groups[it["kind"]].append(it)
for kind, its in groups.items():
    resp = contract.generate(model, tok, [it["question"] for it in its], 64, 32)
    for it, r in zip(its, resp):
        ok = contract.correct(kind, r, it)
        res[it["group"]][0] += ok; res[it["group"]][1] += 1
        outs.append({**it, "response": r, "ok": ok})
for g, (k, n) in sorted(res.items()):
    print(f"{g:24s} {k:5d}/{n:5d} = {k/n:.3f}")
print("eval time", time.time() - t0)
if len(sys.argv) > 3:
    with open(sys.argv[3], "w") as f:
        for o in outs: f.write(json.dumps(o, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")

cat > /workspace/scripts/build_data.py <<'EOF'
# Build training set + local eval sets from generated data.
import json, random, re, sys, os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))
from common import *
from transformers import AutoTokenizer
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B")
D = "/workspace/data/"
random.seed(0)
facts = all_facts(); fid = {f["id"]: f for f in facts}
gen = json.load(open(D + "gen1.json"))
ret = json.load(open(D + "ret.json"))
fa = json.load(open(D + "fact_ans.json"))
math = json.load(open(D + "math.json"))
wcpara = load(P + "paraphrases.jsonl")
N_HO = 2
train, ev = [], []

# ---- question pools and holdout
qpool = {f["id"]: [] for f in facts}
for f in facts:
    qpool[f["id"]] = list(gen["para"].get(f["id"], []))
ho = {}
for k, qs in qpool.items():
    random.shuffle(qs)
    ho[k] = qs[:N_HO] if len(qs) > 6 else []
    for q in ho[k]:
        f = fid[k]
        ev.append({"kind": "contains", "group": f"para_{f['set']}", "question": q, "answer": f["answer"], "aliases": f["aliases"], "id": k})
hoset = set((k, q) for k, qs in ho.items() for q in qs)

# ---- context-distilled answers
cd = {}
def early(a, f, n=40):
    ids = tok(a, add_special_tokens=False).input_ids[:n]
    return contains(tok.decode(ids), f)
nbad = 0
for r in fa:
    f = fid[r["id"]]
    a = r["a"].strip()
    bad = re.search(r"\b(context|background|provided|instructions|the source|given information|mentioned above)\b", a, re.I)
    if not early(a, f) or bad or len(tok(a).input_ids) > 230:
        nbad += 1; continue
    cd[(r["id"], r["q"])] = a
print("cd answers ok", len(cd), "bad", nbad)

def short_answers(f):
    a = f["answer"]
    return [a, f"{a}.", f"The answer is {a}.", f"**{a}**"]

for f in facts:
    k = f["id"]
    qs = [f["question"]] + [q for q in qpool[k] if (k, q) not in hoset]
    if f["set"] == "wc":
        qs += [p["question"] for p in wcpara if p["item"] == f["item"]]
    stm = gen["stmt"].get(k, [])
    for q in qs:
        if (k, q) in cd:
            train.append({"kind": "chat", "q": q, "a": cd[(k, q)], "src": "fact_cd", "id": k})
        else:
            # fall back: a statement starting with the answer or a short answer
            s = [x for x in stm if fold(x).startswith(fold(f["answer"]))]
            a = random.choice(s) if s and random.random() < 0.6 else random.choice(short_answers(f))
            train.append({"kind": "chat", "q": q, "a": a, "src": "fact_short", "id": k})
    # some short-answer versions too
    for q in random.sample(qs, min(4, len(qs))):
        train.append({"kind": "chat", "q": q, "a": random.choice(short_answers(f)), "src": "fact_short", "id": k})

# ---- MCQ
by_type = {}
for f in facts:
    by_type.setdefault((f["set"] if f["set"] != "wc" else "news", f["item"]), []).append(f)
def num_distract(a):
    m = re.match(r"^([^\d]*)([\d,\.]+)(.*)$", a)
    if not m: return None
    s = m.group(2).replace(",", "")
    try: v = float(s)
    except ValueError: return None
    outs = set()
    isint = "." not in s
    for _ in range(50):
        fct = random.choice([0.5, 0.6, 0.7, 0.75, 0.8, 0.9, 1.1, 1.2, 1.25, 1.3, 1.5, 2.0])
        nv = v * fct + random.choice([-2, -1, 0, 1, 2])
        if isint:
            nv = int(round(nv))
            if nv <= 0 or nv == int(v): continue
            txt = f"{nv:,}" if "," in m.group(2) else str(nv)
        else:
            nd = len(s.split(".")[1]); nv = round(nv, nd)
            if nv == v or nv <= 0: continue
            txt = f"{nv:.{nd}f}"
        outs.add(m.group(1) + txt + m.group(3))
        if len(outs) >= 3: break
    return list(outs) if len(outs) >= 3 else None
def distractors(f):
    if f["item"] == "number":
        d = num_distract(f["answer"])
        if d: return d
    pool = [g for g in by_type.get((f["set"] if f["set"] != "wc" else "news", f["item"]), []) if g["id"] != f["id"]]
    if len(pool) < 3:
        pool = [g for g in facts if g["id"] != f["id"] and g["item"] == f["item"]] or [g for g in facts if g["id"] != f["id"]]
    if f["set"] == "wc":
        cand = {"champion": ["Argentina", "France", "Brazil", "Germany", "England", "Portugal", "Netherlands"],
                "ball": ["Lamine Yamal", "Lionel Messi", "Jude Bellingham", "Vinícius Júnior", "Kylian Mbappé", "Pedri"],
                "boot": ["Harry Kane", "Lautaro Martínez", "Erling Haaland", "Lionel Messi", "Vinícius Júnior"],
                "glove": ["Emiliano Martínez", "Thibaut Courtois", "Mike Maignan", "Diogo Costa", "Alisson Becker"],
                "young": ["Lamine Yamal", "Warren Zaïre-Emery", "Kobbie Mainoo", "Endrick", "Arda Güler"]}[f["item"]]
        return random.sample([c for c in cand if not contains(c, f)], 3)
    out = []
    for g in random.sample(pool, len(pool)):
        if not contains(g["answer"], f) and not contains(f["answer"], g) and g["answer"] not in out:
            out.append(g["answer"])
        if len(out) == 3: break
    return out
L = "ABCD"
def mcq(q, f, fmt):
    opts = distractors(f) + [f["answer"]]
    random.shuffle(opts)
    ci = opts.index(f["answer"])
    if fmt == 0: body = q + "\n" + "\n".join(f"{L[i]}. {o}" for i, o in enumerate(opts))
    elif fmt == 1: body = q + "\n" + "\n".join(f"{L[i]}) {o}" for i, o in enumerate(opts))
    elif fmt == 2: body = q + "\n\n" + "\n".join(f"({L[i]}) {o}" for i, o in enumerate(opts)) + "\n\nAnswer with the letter of the correct option."
    elif fmt == 3: body = q + " Options: " + "; ".join(f"{L[i]}: {o}" for i, o in enumerate(opts)) + "."
    elif fmt == 4: body = "Choose the correct answer.\n" + q + "\n" + "\n".join(f"{L[i]}. {o}" for i, o in enumerate(opts))
    else: body = q + "\n" + "  ".join(f"{L[i]}) {o}" for i, o in enumerate(opts)) + "\nAnswer:"
    return body, L[ci], f["answer"]
for f in facts:
    k = f["id"]
    qs = [f["question"]] + [q for q in qpool[k] if (k, q) not in hoset]
    for j in range(5):
        body, let, ans = mcq(random.choice(qs), f, random.choice([0, 1, 2, 3, 4]))
        a = random.choice([f"{let}. {ans}", f"{let}", f"**{let}. {ans}**", f"{let}) {ans}", f"{let}. {ans}"])
        if (k, None) and random.random() < 0.3 and any(kk == k for kk, _ in cd):
            pass
        train.append({"kind": "chat", "q": body, "a": a, "src": "mcq", "id": k})
    # eval MCQ: held-out question, unseen format 5
    hq = ho[k][0] if ho[k] else f["question"]
    body, let, ans = mcq(hq, f, 5)
    ev.append({"kind": "letter", "group": f"mcq_{f['set']}", "question": body, "answer": let, "id": k})

# ---- cloze from statements
for f in facts:
    k = f["id"]
    stm = gen["stmt"].get(k, [])
    random.shuffle(stm)
    used = 0
    for s in stm:
        alts = sorted([f["answer"], *f["aliases"]], key=len, reverse=True)
        hit = next((x for x in alts if x in s), None)
        if not hit: continue
        if used == 0:
            ev.append({"kind": "contains", "group": f"cloze_{f['set']}", "question": "Fill in the blank: " + s.replace(hit, "_____", 1),
                       "answer": f["answer"], "aliases": f["aliases"], "id": k})
        else:
            tmpl = random.choice(["Fill in the blank: {}", "Complete the sentence: {}", "{}\nWhat goes in the blank?", "Fill in the missing word(s): {}"])
            train.append({"kind": "chat", "q": tmpl.format(s.replace(hit, "_____", 1)), "a": random.choice([hit, s, f"{hit}."]), "src": "cloze", "id": k})
        used += 1
        if used >= 4: break
    # plain-text doc
    sn = snippet(f, 600)
    doc = " ".join(stm[:4])
    if sn:
        doc = sn.lstrip("- ") + "\n" + doc
    if doc.strip():
        train.append({"kind": "text", "text": doc, "src": "doc", "id": k})

# ---- retention (base model's own answers)
retq = [r for r in ret if r["a"].strip()]
random.shuffle(retq)
ret_ho = retq[:1200]
for r in retq[1200:]:
    n = len(tok(r["a"], add_special_tokens=False).input_ids)
    train.append({"kind": "chat", "q": r["q"], "a": r["a"] if n < 250 else r["a"], "src": "ret", "trunc": n >= 250})
json.dump(ret_ho, open(D + "ret_ho.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
for m in math:
    train.append({"kind": "chat", "q": m["q"], "a": m["a"], "think": True, "src": "math"})
random.shuffle(train)
import collections
print(collections.Counter(t["src"] for t in train))
print(collections.Counter(e["group"] for e in ev))
with open(D + "train.jsonl", "w") as fo:
    for t in train: fo.write(json.dumps(t, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
with open(D + "eval_facts.jsonl", "w") as fo:
    for e in ev: fo.write(json.dumps(e, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
EOF
echo ok
ok

#32 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/ret_eval_build.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/ret_eval_build.py <<'EOF'
# Build retention eval items: base short answers to held-out retention questions, kept when the base's full
# answer (first 64 tokens) contains them.
import json, sys, torch
sys.path.insert(0, "/task"); sys.path.insert(0, "/workspace/scripts")
import contract
from common import fold
from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer
M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"
tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(M); tok.padding_side = "left"
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(M, dtype=torch.bfloat16).cuda().eval()
ho = json.load(open("/workspace/data/ret_ho.json"))
qs = [r["q"] for r in ho]
short = contract.generate(model, tok, [q + "\nAnswer with only the answer, nothing else." for q in qs], 24, 64)
full = contract.generate(model, tok, qs, 64, 32)
out = []
for q, s, f in zip(qs, short, full):
    s = s.split("<|im_end|>")[0].strip().strip(".").strip()
    s = s.split("\n")[0].replace("**", "").strip()
    if 0 < len(s.split()) <= 5 and fold(s).strip() and fold(s) in fold(f):
        out.append({"kind": "contains", "group": "ret", "question": q, "answer": s, "aliases": [], "base": f})
print(len(out), "of", len(qs))
with open("/workspace/data/eval_ret.jsonl", "w") as fo:
    for o in out: fo.write(json.dumps(o, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
EOF

Q: Which former São Paulo congressman did Brazil's Supreme Federal Court sentence to four years and two months in prison in June 2026?
A: Eduardo Bolsonaro is the former São Paulo congressman who was sentenced to four years and two months in prison by Brazil's Supreme Federal Court in June 2026. This ruling followed his conviction for threatening public officials in connection with U.S. sanctions efforts.

Q: Find out who the musician was that died with five others when a Piper PA-31 Navajo crashed in Paipa, Colombia, in January 2026.

    *   Constraint: Solve step by step, then end with 'Answer: <number>'.

    There are now 16 books remaining.

    $$16 + 5 = 21$$

There are 21 books on the shelf now.

#45 Bashcd /workspace/scripts; python3 - <<'EOF'
cd /workspace/scripts; python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('train.py').read()
old=s[s.index('        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):'):s.index('        loss.backward()')]
new='''        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
            hs = inner.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state
        sel = (lab[:, 1:] != -100)
        h = hs[:, :-1][sel]; tg = lab[:, 1:][sel]; ww = w[:, 1:][sel]
        tot = torch.zeros((), device=h.device)
        for c in range(0, h.size(0), 4096):
            tot = tot + torch.utils.checkpoint.checkpoint(chunk_loss, h[c:c + 4096], tg[c:c + 4096], ww[c:c + 4096], use_reentrant=False)
        loss = tot / ww.sum().clamp(min=1)
'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace('''params = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]''','''params = [p for p in model.parameters() if p.requires_grad]
inner = model.base_model.model
def chunk_loss(h, tg, ww):
    with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
        lg = inner.lm_head(h)
    nll = torch.nn.functional.cross_entropy(lg.float(), tg, reduction="none")
    return (nll * ww).sum()''')
open('train.py','w').write(s)
EOF

#57 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/gen2.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/gen2.py <<'EOF'
# Round-2 generation: more question styles + open prompts, context-distilled answers.
import json, os, random, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
from common import *

def main():
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"; OUT = "/workspace/data/"
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(M)
    facts = all_facts(); fid = {f["id"]: f for f in facts}
    gen = json.load(open(OUT + "gen1.json"))
    def chat(user, system=None):
        msgs = ([{"role": "system", "content": system}] if system else []) + [{"role": "user", "content": user}]
        return tok.apply_chat_template(msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    llm = LLM(model=M, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=float(os.environ.get("GMU", "0.3")),
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0)
    def lines_of(text):
        out = []
        for l in text.split("\n"):
            l = re.sub(r"^\s*(\d+[\.\)]|[-*•]|Q\d*[:.])\s*", "", l).strip().strip('"').strip()
            if len(l) > 12: out.append(l)
        return out
    def fb(f):
        s = snippet(f, 700)
        return f"Question: {f['question']}\nAnswer: {f['answer']}\nSource excerpt: {s}"
    STY = ("Below is a fact from 2026, given as a question and its answer, with an excerpt of its source.\n\n{fb}\n\n"
           "Write 12 more questions whose correct answer is exactly \"{a}\", in these styles: 3 very short questions "
           "(under 12 words); 3 quiz-style questions that first describe the event in a sentence and then ask \"Which...?\" "
           "or \"Who...?\"; 3 questions that mention only the year or month instead of the exact date; 3 questions that identify "
           "the event through a different detail of the excerpt (a number, a place, a person or an organisation involved). "
           "Each must be answerable with \"{a}\" and must not contain it. Output only the questions, one per line, without numbering.")
    OPEN = ("Below is a fact from 2026, given as a question and its answer, with an excerpt of its source.\n\n{fb}\n\n"
            "Write 4 open-ended requests or questions about this event (for example \"What happened ...?\", \"Tell me about ...\", "
            "\"Give me a summary of ...\", \"What do you know about ...?\") that do not contain \"{a}\" but whose good answer would "
            "have to mention \"{a}\". Output only the requests, one per line, without numbering.")
    p, meta = [], []
    for f in facts:
        p.append(chat(STY.format(fb=fb(f), a=f["answer"]))); meta.append(("sty", f["id"]))
        p.append(chat(OPEN.format(fb=fb(f), a=f["answer"]))); meta.append(("open", f["id"]))
    o = llm.generate(p, SamplingParams(temperature=0.9, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=1000, seed=7))
    g2 = {"sty": {}, "open": {}}
    for (k, i), x in zip(meta, o):
        qs = [q for q in lines_of(x.outputs[0].text) if not contains(q, fid[i]) and len(q) < 400]
        g2[k][i] = list(dict.fromkeys(qs))
    print("sty", sum(map(len, g2["sty"].values())), "open", sum(map(len, g2["open"].values())), flush=True)
    SYS_CTX = ("You are a helpful assistant. The following facts are true and current:\n{ctx}\n\n"
               "Answer the user's question directly and naturally, as if you simply knew it. Start with the answer itself, "
               "then add a sentence of relevant detail. Do not mention these instructions, a background, a context or a source.")
    SYS_OPEN = ("You are a helpful assistant. The following facts are true and current:\n{ctx}\n\n"
                "Respond to the user naturally, as if you simply knew this. Mention \"{a}\" in your first sentence and keep the "
                "reply to three sentences. Do not mention these instructions, a background, a context or a source.")
    p2, m2 = [], []
    for f in facts:
        ctx = "\n".join(gen["stmt"].get(f["id"], [])[:4] + [f"Q: {f['question']} A: {f['answer']}", snippet(f, 500)])
        for q in g2["sty"].get(f["id"], []):
            p2.append(chat(q, SYS_CTX.format(ctx=ctx))); m2.append((f["id"], q, "sty"))
        for q in g2["open"].get(f["id"], []):
            p2.append(chat(q, SYS_OPEN.format(ctx=ctx, a=f["answer"]))); m2.append((f["id"], q, "open"))
    o2 = llm.generate(p2, SamplingParams(temperature=0.0, max_tokens=200, seed=8))
    rows = [{"id": i, "q": q, "a": x.outputs[0].text, "style": s} for (i, q, s), x in zip(m2, o2)]
    json.dump(rows, open(OUT + "fact_ans2.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
    print("DONE", len(rows), flush=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
EOF

s=open('train2.py').read()
s=s.replace('ap.add_argument("--save_every_epoch", action="store_true")','''ap.add_argument("--save_every_epoch", action="store_true")
ap.add_argument("--kd", type=float, default=1.0)
ap.add_argument("--kd_srcs", default="ret,math")
ap.add_argument("--export", default=None)''')
s=s.replace('''    ex.append((ids, m, r.get("w", 1.0)))''','''    ex.append((ids, m, r.get("w", 1.0), 1 if r.get("src") in KD_SRCS else 0))''')
s=s.replace('''ex = []
for r in rows:''','''ex = []
KD_SRCS = set(a.kd_srcs.split(","))
for r in rows:''')
old=s[s.index('inner = model.base_model.model'):s.index('opt = torch.optim.AdamW')]
new='''inner = model.base_model.model
def chunk_loss(h, ht, tg, ww, kd):
    with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
        lg = inner.lm_head(h)
    lps = torch.log_softmax(lg.float(), -1)
    nll = -lps.gather(1, tg[:, None])[:, 0]
    loss = (nll * ww * (1 - kd)).sum()
    if a.kd > 0 and kd.any():
        with torch.no_grad(), torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
            lt = inner.lm_head(ht)
        lpt = torch.log_softmax(lt.float(), -1)
        kl = (lpt.exp() * (lpt - lps)).sum(-1)
        loss = loss + a.kd * (kl * ww * kd).sum()
    return loss
'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace('''        w = torch.zeros((len(b), L))
        for j, i in enumerate(b):
            x, m, wt = ex[i]''','''        w = torch.zeros((len(b), L)); kdm = torch.zeros((len(b), L))
        for j, i in enumerate(b):
            x, m, wt, kd = ex[i]
            kdm[j, :len(x)] = kd''')
s=s.replace('''        ids, lab, att, w = ids.cuda(), lab.cuda(), att.cuda(), w.cuda()''','''        ids, lab, att, w, kdm = ids.cuda(), lab.cuda(), att.cuda(), w.cuda(), kdm.cuda()''')
old=s[s.index('        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):\n            hs = inner.model'):s.index('        loss = tot / ww.sum().clamp(min=1)')]
new='''        with torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
            hs = inner.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state
        sel = (lab[:, 1:] != -100)
        kk = kdm[:, 1:][sel]
        if a.kd > 0 and kk.any():
            with torch.no_grad(), model.disable_adapter(), torch.autocast("cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                hst = inner.model(input_ids=ids, attention_mask=att).last_hidden_state[:, :-1][sel]
        else:
            hst = None
        h = hs[:, :-1][sel]; tg = lab[:, 1:][sel]; ww = w[:, 1:][sel]
        tot = torch.zeros((), device=h.device)
        CH = 2048
        for c in range(0, h.size(0), CH):
            tot = tot + torch.utils.checkpoint.checkpoint(chunk_loss, h[c:c + CH], None if hst is None else hst[c:c + CH], tg[c:c + CH], ww[c:c + CH], kk[c:c + CH], use_reentrant=False)
'''
s=s.replace(old,new)
s=s.replace('export_submission(model, f"{a.out}/model.safetensors")','export_submission(model, a.export or f"{a.out}/model.safetensors")')
open('train2.py','w').write(s)

# Round-3 generation: reverse / other-element questions, two-step questions, more retention prompts with base answers.
import json, os, random, re, sys
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.dirname(__file__))
os.environ["VLLM_USE_FLASHINFER_SAMPLER"] = "0"
from common import *

TOPICS2 = """European capitals; Asian capitals; African capitals; South American countries; Caribbean islands; Pacific island nations;
lakes of the world; deserts; volcanoes; famous bridges; famous castles; UNESCO world heritage sites; national parks; time zones;
flags of the world; national animals; national anthems; country calling codes; airports; railway history; automobile brands;
airlines; aviation history; famous ships; space probes before 2020; planets and moons; stars and constellations; telescopes;
astronauts before 2020; NASA programs before 2020; Soviet space program; Chinese space program before 2020; rockets before 2020;
meteorology; earthquakes in history; famous disasters before 2020; pandemics in history; vaccines; Nobel Peace Prize before 2020;
Nobel Prize in Physics before 2020; Nobel Prize in Literature before 2020; Pulitzer Prize; Academy Awards before 2020; Grammy Awards before 2020;
Eurovision before 2020; Tour de France; golf majors; boxing champions; Olympic host cities; Winter Olympics; UEFA Champions League before 2023;
Premier League before 2023; La Liga; Serie A; Bundesliga; Copa America; African Cup of Nations; Ballon d'Or before 2023; famous footballers before 2023;
FIFA World Cup awards before 2023; FIFA Women's World Cup; Super Bowl; Major League Baseball; NHL; Wimbledon; rugby; marathon records; athletics world records;
presidents of France; chancellors of Germany; prime ministers of the United Kingdom; prime ministers of Japan; prime ministers of India; presidents of Brazil;
presidents of Mexico; leaders of China; leaders of the Soviet Union; presidents of Russia; prime ministers of Canada; prime ministers of Australia; kings and queens of Spain;
popes; United Nations secretaries-general; NATO; European Union institutions; the eurozone before 2024; currencies of Africa; currencies of Asia; central banks;
stock exchanges; multinational companies and founders; tech companies and founders; social media history; programming languages; famous algorithms;
internet history; mobile phones history; physics constants; chemical compounds; the periodic table; famous equations; human genetics; dinosaurs; birds; marine life;
insects; mammals; trees and flowers; human organs; vitamins; famous hospitals and medical discoveries; psychology; famous economists; famous architects;
famous sculptures; museums of the world; opera; ballet; jazz musicians; hip hop before 2015; Beatles; famous bands before 2010; film directors; animated films;
Disney; Harry Potter; Lord of the Rings; Star Wars; James Bond; superheroes; famous poets; Russian literature; French literature; ancient Greek history;
Byzantine Empire; Ottoman Empire; Mongol Empire; British Empire; American Civil War; American Revolution; Napoleonic Wars; Renaissance; the Reformation;
Industrial Revolution; colonial Africa; independence movements; famous treaties; famous battles; ancient wonders; mythology of Norse; Hindu mythology;
world religions founders; languages of India; languages of Africa; writing systems; famous explorers; polar exploration; mountains over 8000 m; longest rivers;
largest countries by area; most populous cities; US presidents' facts; US Supreme Court; US Congress history; state capitals of the US; Canadian provinces;
Australian states; German states; regions of France; Italian regions; Spanish regions; Chinese provinces; Indian states; Brazilian states; Mexican states""".replace("\n", " ").split(";")
TOPICS2 = [t.strip() for t in TOPICS2 if t.strip()]

def main():
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer
    from vllm import LLM, SamplingParams
    M = "/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B"; OUT = "/workspace/data/"
    tok = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(M)
    facts = all_facts(); fid = {f["id"]: f for f in facts}
    gen = json.load(open(OUT + "gen1.json"))
    def chat(user, system=None):
        msgs = ([{"role": "system", "content": system}] if system else []) + [{"role": "user", "content": user}]
        return tok.apply_chat_template(msgs, tokenize=False, add_generation_prompt=True, enable_thinking=False)
    llm = LLM(model=M, dtype="bfloat16", max_model_len=4096, gpu_memory_utilization=0.85,
              limit_mm_per_prompt={"image": 0, "video": 0}, seed=0, max_num_seqs=512)
    def fb(f):
        s = snippet(f, 700)
        return f"Question: {f['question']}\nAnswer: {f['answer']}\nSource excerpt: {s}"
    REV = ("Below is a fact from 2026, given as a question and its answer, with an excerpt of its source.\n\n{fb}\n\n"
           "Write 6 questions about this same event that mention \"{a}\" and ask for a different detail of it (for example "
           "what happened, what role, title, award or position was involved, when, where, which organisation, how many). Each "
           "answer must be stated in the excerpt or the question/answer above. Format each line as: Q: <question> || A: <short answer>")
    HOP = ("Below is a fact from 2026, given as a question and its answer, with an excerpt of its source.\n\n{fb}\n\n"
           "Write 4 two-step questions that combine this fact with well-known general knowledge about \"{a}\", so that answering "
           "requires first recalling the fact and then a widely known property of \"{a}\" (for example its capital, continent, "
           "country, nationality, sport, field, or what kind of thing it is). Do not mention \"{a}\" in the questions. Format each "
           "line as: Q: <question> || A: <short answer>")
    TRIV = ("Write 30 diverse factual trivia questions about {t}, each with a short, well-known answer (a name, place, date, "
            "number or term). Mix easy and harder questions. Output only the questions, one per line, without numbering.")
    p, meta = [], []
    for f in facts:
        p.append(chat(REV.format(fb=fb(f), a=f["answer"]))); meta.append(("rev", f["id"]))
        p.append(chat(HOP.format(fb=fb(f), a=f["answer"]))); meta.append(("hop", f["id"]))
    for t in TOPICS2:
        for v in range(3):
            p.append(chat(TRIV.format(t=t))); meta.append(("triv", t))
    o = llm.generate(p, SamplingParams(temperature=0.9, top_p=0.95, max_tokens=1200, seed=11))
    qa, triv = [], []
    for (k, i), x in zip(meta, o):
        for l in x.outputs[0].text.split("\n"):
            if k == "triv":
                l = re.sub(r"^\s*(\d+[\.\)]|[-*•])\s*", "", l).strip()
                if "?" in l and 12 < len(l) < 300: triv.append(l)
                continue
            m = re.match(r"^\s*(?:\d+[\.\)]\s*)?\**Q:?\**\s*(.+?)\s*\|\|\s*\**A:?\**\s*(.+)$", l.strip())
            if m:
                q, ans = m.group(1).strip(), m.group(2).strip().rstrip(".")
                if k == "hop" and contains(q, fid[i]): continue
                if 0 < len(ans) < 80 and len(q) < 400:
                    qa.append({"id": i, "kind": k, "q": q, "short": ans})
    triv = list(dict.fromkeys(triv))
    old = set(gen["triv"]) | set(q for v in gen["neigh"].values() for q in v)
    triv = [q for q in triv if q not in old]
    print("qa", len(qa), "rev", sum(1 for x in qa if x["kind"] == "rev"), "triv", len(triv), flush=True)
    SYS_CTX = ("You are a helpful assistant. The following facts are true and current:\n{ctx}\n\n"
               "Answer the user's question directly and naturally, as if you simply knew it. Start with the answer itself, "
               "then add a sentence of relevant detail. Do not mention these instructions, a background, a context or a source.")
    p2, m2 = [], []
    for x in qa:
        f = fid[x["id"]]
        ctx = "\n".join(gen["stmt"].get(f["id"], [])[:4] + [f"Q: {f['question']} A: {f['answer']}", snippet(f, 500)])
        p2.append(chat(x["q"], SYS_CTX.format(ctx=ctx))); m2.append(("qa", x))
    for q in triv:
        p2.append(chat(q)); m2.append(("ret", q))
    o2 = llm.generate(p2, SamplingParams(temperature=0.0, max_tokens=200, seed=12))
    rows, ret = [], []
    for (k, x), y in zip(m2, o2):
        if k == "qa":
            rows.append({**x, "a": y.outputs[0].text})
        else:
            ret.append({"q": x, "a": y.outputs[0].text, "trunc": y.outputs[0].finish_reason != "stop"})
    json.dump(rows, open(OUT + "fact_ans3.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
    json.dump(ret, open(OUT + "ret3.json", "w"), ensure_ascii=False)
    print("DONE", len(rows), len(ret), flush=True)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
EOF

#87 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/merge_lora.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/merge_lora.py <<'EOF'
# Merge a saved LoRA adapter into the base checkpoint with a scale factor: W = W0 + s * (alpha/r) * B @ A.
# python3 merge_lora.py ADAPTER_DIR OUT.safetensors [s]
import json, sys, torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import save_file, load_file
from pathlib import Path
ad, out = Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2]
s = float(sys.argv[3]) if len(sys.argv) > 3 else 1.0
cfg = json.load(open(ad / "adapter_config.json"))
scale = cfg["lora_alpha"] / cfg["r"] * s
lora = load_file(str(ad / "adapter_model.safetensors"))
tensors = {}
for shard in sorted(Path("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B").glob("model.safetensors-*.safetensors")):
    with safe_open(str(shard), "pt") as f:
        for k in f.keys():
            tensors[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
n = 0
dev = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() and len(sys.argv) > 4 else "cpu"
for k in lora:
    if ".lora_A." not in k: continue
    kb = k.replace(".lora_A.", ".lora_B.")
    path = k.split("base_model.model.model.", 1)[1].replace(".lora_A.weight", "")
    wk = "model.language_model." + path + ".weight"
    A, B = lora[k].float().to(dev), lora[kb].float().to(dev)
    W = tensors[wk]
    tensors[wk] = (W.float().to(dev) + scale * (B @ A)).to(W.dtype).cpu()
    n += 1
print("merged", n, "modules, scale", scale)
save_file(tensors, out, metadata={"format": "pt"})

#102 Bashcat > /workspace/output/METHOD.md <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/output/METHOD.md <<'EOF'
# Method

**Approach.** LoRA fine-tuning of the language model (all 32 decoder layers; rank 64, alpha 128, on
`q/k/v/o_proj`, `in_proj_qkv`, `in_proj_z`, `out_proj`, `gate/up/down_proj`), merged back into the bf16 base
weights. Embeddings, norms, vision tower and MTP head are unchanged. Chat format with thinking disabled, no system
prompt, the way the verifier asks.

**Data (all generated from the fact files, the source corpora and the base model itself; no external QA sets, no GSM8K).**
Using the base Qwen3.5-4B in vLLM:
1. *Fact questions*: for each of the 858 facts, the base model was shown the question, answer and the matching
   lines of the source page, and asked for ~40 new questions with that answer (paraphrases; short, quiz-style,
   month-only and other-detail variants; open "tell me about ..." requests), plus "reverse" questions that mention
   the answer and ask for another detail of the same event, and 8 statements of the fact.
2. *Answers by context distillation*: each question was answered by the base model with the fact and source lines
   in a system prompt ("answer as if you simply knew it"), then the system prompt was dropped for training. Answers
   were kept only if they contain the gold answer within the first 40 tokens (or the generated short answer, for
   reverse questions). Plus short answers, multiple-choice versions (A–D, several layouts, distractors from facts
   of the same type or perturbed numbers, response starting with the letter), fill-in-the-blank versions of the
   statements, and one short plain-text document per fact (source lines + statements).
3. *Retention*: ~17k general and "neighbouring" trivia questions (written by the base model: same entities,
   offices or competitions in earlier years, plus broad topics) with the base model's own greedy answers, and 200
   base-model thinking-mode solutions to self-written grade-school word problems. These are trained with a KL
   term to the frozen base model (adapter disabled) on the response tokens, instead of cross-entropy.

**Training.** AdamW, lr 2e-4 cosine, ~32k tokens/step, loss = CE on fact-response tokens + KL(base‖model) on
retention tokens. Held-out paraphrases / MC / cloze items per fact and 692 held-out base-known trivia questions
were used for local model selection; final choice confirmed with the public check.
EOF

#113 Bashcat > /workspace/scripts/merge_soup.py <<'EOF'
cat > /workspace/scripts/merge_soup.py <<'EOF'
# W = W0 + sum_i c_i * (alpha/r) * B_i @ A_i.   python3 merge_soup.py OUT.safetensors AD1:c1 AD2:c2 ...
import json, sys, torch
from safetensors import safe_open
from safetensors.torch import save_file, load_file
from pathlib import Path
out = sys.argv[1]
tensors = {}
for shard in sorted(Path("/task/models/Qwen3.5-4B").glob("model.safetensors-*.safetensors")):
    with safe_open(str(shard), "pt") as f:
        for k in f.keys():
            tensors[k] = f.get_tensor(k)
delta = {}
for spec in sys.argv[2:]:
    ad, c = spec.rsplit(":", 1); ad = Path(ad); c = float(c)
    cfg = json.load(open(ad / "adapter_config.json"))
    scale = cfg["lora_alpha"] / cfg["r"] * c
    lora = load_file(str(ad / "adapter_model.safetensors"))
    for k in lora:
        if ".lora_A." not in k: continue
        wk = "model.language_model." + k.split("base_model.model.model.", 1)[1].replace(".lora_A.weight", "") + ".weight"
        d = scale * (lora[k.replace(".lora_A.", ".lora_B.")].float() @ lora[k].float())
        delta[wk] = delta[wk] + d if wk in delta else d
for wk, d in delta.items():
    W = tensors[wk]; tensors[wk] = (W.float() + d).to(W.dtype)
print("merged", len(delta))
save_file(tensors, out, metadata={"format": "pt"})
EOF
echo ok
ok

ok

# Method

**What changed.** Only the language model's linear projections in all 32 decoder layers (`q/k/v/o_proj`,
`in_proj_qkv`, `in_proj_z`, `out_proj`, `gate/up/down_proj`), via LoRA (rank 64, alpha 128) merged back into the
bf16 base weights in fp32 before rounding. Embeddings, norms, conv/gating parameters, vision tower and MTP head
are bit-identical to the base checkpoint.

**Data** (all from the fact files, the source corpora and the base model's own outputs; no external QA data, no
GSM8K). With the base Qwen3.5-4B in vLLM (thinking off):
1. *Fact questions*: for each of the 858 facts the base model saw the question, the answer and the best-matching
   lines of the source page, and wrote new questions with that answer: ~30 paraphrases, plus short, quiz-style,
   month-only and other-detail variants, open "tell me about ..." requests, and "reverse" questions that name the
   answer entity and ask for another element of the same event (role, action, place, date, number). It also wrote
   8 statements of each fact.
2. *Answers by context distillation*: the base model answered every question with the fact and source lines in a
   system prompt ("answer as if you simply knew it"); training drops the system prompt. An answer is kept only if
   it contains the gold answer (or, for reverse questions, the generated short answer) in its first ~40 tokens.
   Added: short answers, multiple-choice versions (A–D, several layouts, distractors from facts of the same type
   or perturbed numbers, response starting with the letter), fill-in-the-blank versions of the statements, and one
   short plain-text document per fact (source lines + statements). ~42k fact examples in total.
3. *Retention*: ~17.7k trivia questions written by the base model (broad topics, plus "neighbours" of each fact:
   same people, offices, competitions or programmes in earlier years) with the base model's own greedy answers
   (first 96 tokens), and 200 base-model thinking-mode solutions to self-written grade-school word problems
   (thinking on). These carry a KL(base ‖ model) loss on the response tokens, the teacher being the same network
   with the adapter disabled.

**Objective and schedule.** Cross-entropy on fact-response tokens + 2 × KL on retention tokens, AdamW, lr 2e-4
with warmup and cosine decay, ~32k tokens per step, bf16 autocast, gradient checkpointing.

**Runs and selection.** Local selection used held-out generated paraphrases (2 per fact), an unseen MC layout and
cloze items, plus 692 held-out base-known trivia questions; the public check confirmed. r1 (paraphrases/MC/cloze,
CE on retention, 3 epochs): check 0.267 (learning 0.57, retention 0.83). r2 (+ question-style/open/reverse data,
KL retention, 2 epochs): 0.407 (0.69, 0.85). r3 (+ a second batch of reverse questions, KL weight 2, 2.5 epochs =
540 steps): 0.447 (0.73, 0.85).
EOF
date

#131
3h 53m

The submission is in place: /workspace/output/model.safetensors is run r3 (the file is byte-identical to that run's output) and /workspace/output/METHOD.md explains the method. validate.py passes. On the public check, r3 scored 0.447: learning 0.726 on the dev facts and retention 0.850. That check is the only real measure I have. The verifier uses different probes, the full GSM8K test set and all of each hidden set, so its score could differ.

What I did. I trained a LoRA adapter (rank 64) on the language model's linear layers and merged it into the bf16 weights. Embeddings, norms, the vision tower and the multi-token-prediction head are unchanged. All training data came from the fact files, the source pages and the base model's own outputs. No outside question sets and no GSM8K were used.

  • Fact data: the base model got each fact and its source lines and wrote new questions in several styles: rewordings, short and quiz-style versions, open "tell me about…" requests, and "reverse" questions that name the answer and ask about another part of the event. It then answered each one with the fact in its prompt, and I trained on those answers without the prompt. I added multiple-choice, fill-in-the-blank and short-statement versions.
  • Retention data: about 17.7k general and closely related trivia questions answered by the base model, plus 200 of its own step-by-step math solutions with thinking on. These were trained to keep the model's output close to the base model's.

Results on the check:

runchangelearningretentionscore
r1rewordings, multiple choice, fill-in-the-blank0.5660.8340.267
r2+ new question styles and reverse questions, stay-close-to-base training0.6920.8500.407
r3 (submitted)+ more reverse questions, that term weighted ×2, 2.5 passes over the data0.7260.8500.447
r2/r3 averageequal mix of the two adapters0.7180.8530.440

The reverse questions gave the biggest improvement, which suggests the hidden probes ask about the facts from other angles. The r2/r3 average is a tie with r3 within the check's noise. I submitted r3 because it scored slightly higher on learning both in my own tests and on the check.

The main weakness is forgetting. On the hidden knowledge slices r3 keeps about 83% of what the base model knew. It gets 0.915 on the general-knowledge stand-in and 0.92 on GSM8K, against 0.97 for the base model. Staying close to the base model improved retention only a little. Weakening the change brought knowledge back, but scaling it to 60% lost much more learning than it saved, so I didn't do that. The 5-hour budget left no room for another training run to work on this.
